## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我把某个仓库里挂掉的测试修好。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くことを保証する」通信、UDPは「速さを優先して、届くかどうかは保証しない」通信です。

TCPは相手と接続を確立してから送り、届いたか・順番どおりかを確認して、欠けたものは再送します。ウェブ閲覧やメールのように正確さが大事な用途向けです。UDPは確認も再送もせず、データをそのまま送るので遅延が少なく、動画通話やオンラインゲームのように多少の欠落より遅れを避けたい用途に向いています。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, porque deja un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o pública, porque no reescribe commits que otros pueden tener.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接的：数据有确认和重传，按序送达；UDP 是不可靠的、无连接的：只管尽快发出去，不保证到达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours and private, so you can tidy its commits into a clean history before anyone else builds on them. Merge when the branch is shared or already public, because rebasing rewrites commits others may have pulled.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行:

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

如果只想跑某一个测试用例,用 `-t` 按名字过滤:

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts -t "应该忽略空行"
```

另外,`bun test` 会把路径里包含这段文字的测试文件都跑一遍,所以写个文件名的一部分也可以,比如 `bun test parse`。
```

背景：用户周六（2026-11-28）去北京参加 AGI Bar 晚上的活动（地址：北京市海淀区中关村创业大街2号楼A号底商），当晚住一晚，周日回来。住宿已选朗丽兹（北京中关村苏州街店），备选汉庭（中关村苏州街地铁站店）。

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a durable file before the change is applied to the main data. If the system crashes midway, the database replays or rolls back the log on restart, so committed work survives and partial work doesn't corrupt anything.
```

### noticed-how-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你怎么知道我要去 AGI Bar 的？是哪天来着
  [partner message] 我是从你的日历看到的：上面写着「AGI Bar Beijing talk」，日期是 11/25（周三）。我还查了 AGI Bar 的官网，写的也是 11/25 周三，19:00 开门。

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with the nightly hawdb write results. Night 3 shows 182k writes/s, and it adds each new night as the benchmark reports.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1760 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest write throughput</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="tp"><span class="k-none">Not run yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Nightly hawdb write benchmark, every night at 23:00</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">Nothing yet. Each night's result will show here.</div>
</div>
</main>
<script>
function render(runs) {
  runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  var tp = document.getElementById('tp');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!runs.length) {
    empty.hidden = false;
    tp.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">Not run yet</span>';
    return;
  }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
  tp.textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
  sub.textContent = last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
  runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = r.night;
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = 'p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · stalls ' + r.stalls;
    var v = document.createElement('div');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.textContent = r.writes + '/s';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(s);
    row.appendChild(v);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(render);
ducter.onChange(render);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];

  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记了花销就会合计在这里";
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");

    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(sub);row.appendChild(val);
    ul.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}

  if(!what||!(yuan>0))return;
  const xs=(ducter.get("expenses")||[]).slice();
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
  document.getElementById("day").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();

</div>

<script>
let feeds = [];
let editing = null;
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const p = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
function todayKey() { const d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate()); }
function nowHM() { const d = new Date(); return p(d.getHours()) + ':' + p(d.getMinutes()); }
function todays() { return feeds.filter(f => f.date === todayKey()).sort((a, b) => a.time < b.time ? -1 : 1); }

function render() {
  const li … list.length ? '今天 ' + list.length + ' 次,合计 ' + total + 'ml' : '今天还没记');

function reset() {
  editing = null;
  $('t').value = nowHM();
  $('ml').value = '';
  $('del').hidden = true;
}

function edit(id) {
  const f = feeds.find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!f) return;
  editing = id;
  $('t').value = f.time;
  $('ml').value = f.ml;
  $('del').hidden = false;
  $('addbox').open = true;
}

$('save').onclick = () => {
  const ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
  const raw = $('t').value.trim();
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(raw) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  const [h, m] = raw.split(':');
  const time = p(h) + ':' + m;
  if (editing !== null) {
    const f = feeds.find(x => x.id === editing);
    if (f) { f.time = time; f.ml = ml; }
  } else {
    feeds.push({ id: Date.now(), date: todayKey(), time: time, ml: ml });
  }
  ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  reset();
  $('addbox').open = false;
  render();
};

$('del').onclick = () => {
  feeds = feeds.filter(x => x.id !== editing);
  ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  reset();
  $('addbox').open = false;
  render();
};

ducter.onChange(() => { feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || []; render(); });
feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
reset();
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"date":"2026-11-23","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"date":"2026-11-23","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

<style>
.pr-row{width:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;text-align:left;background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:8px;color:var(--d-ink);font:inherit;cursor:pointer}
.pr-box{flex:none;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;border:2px solid var(--d-muted);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:14px;color:#fff}
.pr-on .pr-box{background:var(--d-good);border-color:var(--d-good)}
.pr-on .pr-title{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
.pr-main{flex:1;min-width:0}
.pr-title{font-weight:600;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.pr-sub{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted);margin-top:2px}
</style>
<script>
var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];
var done = {};

function openCount() {
  return ITEMS.filter(function (i) { return !done[i.id]; }).length;
}

function render() {
  var open = openCount();
  document.getElementById('n').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open === 0 ? 'All landed' : 'Tap a PR when it lands';
  document.getElementById('list').innerHTML = ITEMS.map(function (i) {
    var on = !!done[i.id];
    return '<button class="pr-row' + (on ? ' pr-on' : '') + '" data-id="' + i.id + '">' +
      '<span class="pr-box">' + (on ? '✓' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="pr-main"><span class="pr-title" style="display:block">' + i.title + ' · ' + i.what + '</span>' +
      '<span class="pr-sub" style="display:block">' + i.who + '</span></span>' +
      '</button>';
  }).join('');
  ducter.summary(open === 0 ? 'All landed' : open + ' waiting');
}

document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
  var b = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
  if (!b) return;
  var id = b.getAttribute('data-id');
  if (done[id]) { delete done[id]; } else { done[id] = true; }
  ducter.set('done', done);
  render();
});

function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (v) {
    done = v || {};
    render();
  });
}

ducter.onChange(load);
load();
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412","what":"Compaction cut","who":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Mon"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","what":"Retry on 502","who":"CI failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tue"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418","what":"Apps bar","who":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我在 TTL 那个测试的改动上补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，补好告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

要求：
- 在现有的 TTL 测试改动上（同一分支）补一条测试，覆盖缓存项正好到期那一刻的行为（假时钟推进到恰好等于 TTL 的时间点）。
- 明确断言：到期时刻本身的读取结果（是否视为已过期）与代码实际语义一致，并在测试名或注释里写清楚边界是包含还是不包含。
- 保持假时钟，不使用真实 sleep 或真实计时。
- 跑一遍整个测试文件，并连续跑 20 次确认稳定。

范围外：
- 不合并、不推送、不开 PR。
- 不改 values() 等其他功能的代码。

完成后报告：新增测试的名字、断言的边界语义，以及测试运行的输出（通过数、20 次连跑结果）。
```
